# KTM 65 SX 2024: aftermarket parts

1154 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 74 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1080 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 0 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
